Only a copy that is frozen (carried **and** not PLANNED, `isCarriedFrozen`) counts as "kept from the previous version": the "kept" badge on the plan screen, "KEPT FROM PREVIOUS VERSION" on the driver sheets, "(kept from previous version)" in the workbook and the change summary's "locked/dispatched loads preserved" (review of PR3: the PLANNED copies were labelled kept and counted too);
   - copies the parent's **chosen** option (`ScenarioResult`) with its `UnservedOrder` rows (not the alternatives: they were computed around the parent's loads); the copy's `scope.frozenLoadIds` name the copied loads. It also copies `summaryJson`, `reconciliationJson`, `totalOrders` and `unservedCount`, and sets the child's `chosenScenarioId` to the copy. The child's status follows its loads: READY, or DISPATCHED when every load is out. Null Json columns are copied as SQL NULL by `copyRowData` (`lib/dispatch/prisma-copy.ts`), which finds a model's Json columns from the Prisma schema;
   - computes the child's own timetable check (`refreshFeasibility`, PR4);
   - marks the parent `SUPERSEDED` with `supersededAt`;
   - writes audit `PLAN_VERSION_CREATED` (`frozenLoadsCarried`, `loadsCopied`, `planCopied`);
7. `startDispatchOptimize(child, { freshVersion, ticket, now })`. In `buildDispatchRequest(child)`, PLANNED loads are ignored as before, the copied frozen loads keep their orders and cases, each truck's `frozen_trips` are fixed, and `previous_truck_id` is sent only when `usesPlanContinuity` is true. On the delivery day itself the request starts from now + preparation (see "Same-day re-plans" below). The background job then runs exactly as in [3.6](#36-step-4-optimize), on the child: on success `persistDispatchResult` deletes the copied option and `applyScenario` replaces the copied PLANNED loads, and `refreshPlanFacts` writes the change summary against the parent.

**Same-day re-plans start from now (PR8, scenario finding S04 / N2).** Before PR8 RouteIQ had no notion of the current time: a late order re-planned at 09:00 for today was put on new loads leaving at 06:00 (5 of 10 new loads in the scenario run left before 09:00, and 5 of 6 late clinics were "delivered" before their orders existed). Now, when an OPTIMIZE or a re-plan is built on its own delivery day (company timezone), no new load leaves the depot before **now + the turnaround between loads** (`reloadMinutes`, 30 min for NMWC): `buildDispatchRequest` sends that as `shift_start_min` ([3.6](#36-step-4-optimize)), and the time the plan is made as `loading_from_min` (PR8 review, below). The optimizer then:
- lets every truck's first new load leave at the later of that time, the depot opening and the truck's own availability ([4.6](#46-truck-days-and-prefilters));
- counts the loading per case from now on every truck (PR8 review): a new load leaves no earlier than now + turnaround + `loadingMinPerCase` x its cases, on a truck standing at the depot exactly as on one coming back;
- locked, loading and dispatched loads keep their times; a truck that is still out (for example back at 09:57) leaves again only after its return + turnaround + loading minutes per case of the next load, as before;
- the early-arrival preference for P1 / P2 is measured from that time instead of 06:00, and so is the early part of the options table's **Preference cost** (PR7, `preference_penalties.early`). *What it gains* shows two things: the P1/P2 minutes-earlier figure, which compares service starts between options and so does not depend on the start, and the preference-cost difference, whose early part counts from the same-day start like the column;
- a customer whose hard window closes before any truck can get there is unserved `HARD_WINDOW_INFEASIBLE` with its earliest possible arrival;
- when now + preparation is at or after the depot's closing time, the warning says no new load can leave today, and every open order is unserved `SHIFT_LIMIT` for that reason ("Planned on the delivery day from 18:15: the depot closes at 18:00, so no new load can leave today.", PR8 rebase review, [4.6](#46-truck-days-and-prefilters)), not as if the trucks were taken up by locked or dispatched loads.

The plan says so: a plan warning ("Planned from 09:30 (now 09:00 + 30 min preparation): the plan is for today, so no new load leaves the depot before 09:30. ..."; the first of the optimizer's warnings, but notes about outdated weights, changed master data or drivers, and the timing warnings, are shown above it), the ASSUMPTIONS row "Planned from (plan made on the delivery day)" (from `PlanSettings.planFrom`, kept with the option), and `planFromMin` in the `OPTIMIZE_STARTED` audit row. The new loads are timed with 09:30 as their first departure (`rulesFrom`, the truck snapshot), so the dispatch gate checks them against it; a carried load keeps the rules it was planned with. "Now" is taken when the optimization is started (the request is built then), not when a queued job reaches the solver. Loads that already left but are still PLANNED in RouteIQ are planned again from now: dispatch them before re-planning. A plan for a future day is unchanged; a plan for a past day is built as before.

**Loading starts now too (PR8 review, scenario review finding "same-day preparation leaves out the loading minutes per case").** With only `shift_start_min` = now + turnaround, the optimizer added the loading minutes per case only after a frozen return (`frozen_return_s`): a truck idle all morning, or one back before now, left at 09:30 with a 700-case load whose 35 min of loading were never counted, while a truck back at 09:00 left at 10:05 with the same load. Now `buildDispatchRequest` also sends `loading_from_min` (the time the plan is made, `planDayNowMin`) whenever the plan is built on its delivery day, and the solver treats it like a frozen return for the loading gap, without using a trip, moving the shift anchor or changing driver pay: `TruckDay.ready_s` = the later of the last frozen return and that time, used by the route search's start bound, the repack's departure range (`load_repack._first_departure_s`), the exact timing and `timing_ok` ([4.6](#46-truck-days-and-prefilters)). The solver's own check (`feasibility.py`) and the web's dispatch gate (`feasibility.ts`, `PlanRules.loadingFromMin` from the truck snapshot) check the same rule (`TURNAROUND`, "... the plan was made at 09:00 on its delivery day, so loading starts then: the truck needs 65 min to reload and load 700 cases: ready 10:05"); where it is later than the previous return or the first departure, only it is reported. The same rule holds on a plan made on the delivery day before its first departure (05:15 for 06:00: no "Planned from", but a full truck may leave after 06:00); the plan then warns "Planned on the delivery day at 05:15: loading starts now, ..." when a full truck of the depot would be later than the first departure, and ASSUMPTIONS has a "Loading from (plan made on the delivery day)" row. The "Planned from" warning and ASSUMPTIONS row name the loading per case ("... Loading starts now too, so each new load also waits for its own loading time, 0.04 min per case: a full 800-case truck leaves at 10:02 at the earliest."). A solver without the field ignores it (Pydantic drops unknown fields): its plans are then refused by the web gate where they break the rule, so deploy the solver first.

**A failed re-plan keeps the previous plan.** If the child's optimization fails (solver error or timeout, the solver finding no plan, a deploy killing the solve and the janitor reaping it), the child is FAILED but still holds the previous plan intact: its loads can be locked, loaded and dispatched (its reconciliation is the parent's), and the screen shows "Optimization failed - previous plan kept". Weights entered under Products since are not saved (the copied loads keep matching their orders, and the "planned with the old weight" warning stays). A load change turns it READY. Re-plan again to retry: that creates the next version; the FAILED version itself is never optimized again in place (409 `NEW_VERSION_REQUIRED`). If the start itself is refused after the version was created (for example `LOADS_CHANGED` or `PLAN_BUSY`), the answer carries `previousPlanKept: true` and the new version stays READY with the copy.

**Bring forward: orders not delivered on earlier days (PR9).** Before PR9 an order that was not delivered stayed on its own delivery date: the next day's plan never saw it. Now Step 1 of the day screen for day D and a depot shows "Not delivered on earlier days: N orders (M cases)" (`carry-over-panel.tsx`) with a list and "Bring forward to <D>" (PLANNER, like confirming a file and a late order); when D is later than today, today's own leftovers follow in a group of their own, "Today (27 Sep) - may still leave today", unticked (owner decision).

- **What is listed** (`GET /api/dispatch/carry-over?date&depotId` → `carryOverPreview` → `carryCandidates` in `lib/dispatch/carry-over.ts`, pure): the orders of the tenant and depot (like `ordersInScopeWhere`; every order has a depot since audit PR A5) with a delivery date in [D-7, D-1] - **never after the company's today** (`carryWindow`, in the tenant timezone; the PR9 review had capped it at yesterday, the owner's decision on today's orders moved the cap to today) - not carried already and not `DISPATCHED` / `DELIVERED`, whose cases were not delivered. Per order line: its cases minus the cases on loads of its day's **live plan** (`currentPlan`) that left the depot (`DISPATCHED` or `COMPLETED`; a split part by its `portionLinesJson`). So an order unserved in that plan (`UnservedOrder` of the option in use), on a load that never left (`PLANNED`, `LOCKED` or `LOADING`), or of a day with no plan (or never optimized, or added after the plan was made) is listed with only its open cases, each with why (`UNSERVED` with the reason, `NOT_LEFT` with the loads, `NEVER_PLANNED`). Orders on loads that left are delivered and never listed. **Today's orders** (owner decision after the PR9 reviews: NMWC loads tomorrow's trucks tonight after 20:00, and the dispatcher plans tomorrow in the evening, today's leftovers included): when D is later than today (tomorrow, the day screen's default), today's orders qualify exactly like an earlier day's - on a load of today that has not left (`PLANNED`, `LOCKED`, `LOADING`: "has not left the depot yet"), unserved in today's live plan, or not planned yet; never on a `DISPATCHED` or `COMPLETED` load - and are marked `ofToday`. The panel lists them after the earlier days in a group of their own, **"Today (27 Sep) - may still leave today"**, with the warning "Today's loads that have not left yet may still go out today; tick only orders you know will not be delivered today." (`carryTodayTitle`, `CARRY_TODAY_WARNING`; the warning also shows with the list closed), **unticked by default**; the earlier days stay ticked. The preview counts them apart (`todayOrders` / `todayCases`; `orders` / `cases` are the earlier days'). An order is never brought forward to its own day (D is always later than the order's date: for D = today the window ends yesterday), and orders of a day not due yet are never listed (before the PR9 review, D = 30 Sep listed 28 and 29 Sep as "never planned", ticked). `bringForward` reads the same window in its transaction.
- **Listed but not carried** (`blocked`, with the reason on screen): a deactivated customer (`CUSTOMER_INACTIVE`); a sales-order line already confirmed for D (`ALREADY_ON_DAY`: the file intake's duplicate identity `lineDupKey`, so a line entered again for D is never added twice); a sales-order line that is also on an order of a **later delivery date** (`SAME_LINE_LATER`, PR9 second review): the same identity without the date (sales order, customer and branch, product) on any order after the candidate's own date, in **any depot and any state** - open, brought forward, dispatched or delivered - read from the intake keys of those sales orders and the lines of the same customers' later orders (`laterLinesOf`), in the preview and inside `bringForward` alike. Only the newest open order with the line is carried ("Sales order SO-9 (W500) is also open on 26 Sep: only that order is brought forward."); none when the newer one was brought forward, dispatched or delivered, or is on today or a day still to come ("... was entered again for 26 Sep (brought forward to 27 Sep): not brought forward, so it is not delivered twice."). Before the second review only open orders of the window and the lines on D counted, so once the newer order was carried to 27 Sep (or dispatched), opening 28 Sep offered the older one again, ticked, and the line was delivered and invoiced twice. A day whose plan is `OPTIMIZING` (`DAY_OPTIMIZING`).
- **The selection** (`carry-over-panel.tsx`): every order of an earlier day that can be carried is ticked, none of today (`carryTickedByDefault`). The panel keeps what the dispatcher ticked or unticked by hand (`CarryChoices`; `carrySelected` / `toggleCarry` in `carry-view.ts`, PR9 second review, extended for today's orders), not the ticked set, so reading the list again - after a 409, a partial bring forward, an OPTIMIZE or RE-PLAN of the day, a new file, **Look again** - never ticks an unticked order again, never ticks an order of today by itself, and keeps one the dispatcher ticked; an order new to the list gets its default. Another day or depot starts afresh. The button shows the count whenever the selection is not "every earlier order" (with how many of today; `carryButtonSuffix`), each row's reason is labelled by `carryWhyLabel` ("Load not left yet" / "Not planned" for today's orders, never "Load never left" / "Never planned", which would contradict "may still leave today"), the question before it names today's orders (`carryConfirmText`, given the listed orders - their `ofToday` is required by its type, so the POST body, which says `today`, cannot be passed by mistake): they are closed on today (today's plan stays as it is, for the record) and a load of today still holding one cannot be locked, loaded or dispatched today - never "they come off today's plan", which the plan rows contradict. The warning above today's group shows also with the list closed. The toast afterwards says how many were today's and to re-plan today or unlock their loads (a loading one goes Back to locked first; `carryDoneText`). The button is off while D's plan is being optimized (or queued).
- **Bring forward** (`POST /api/dispatch/carry-over { date, depotId, selected: [{ orderId, cases, today? }] }` → `bringForward`; `cases` is what the list showed, the expected state; `today: true` marks an order ticked in the Today group). **An order of today is carried only when it is sent with `today: true`**: sent without it - as "select every order", or a screen opened before this change, would - it answers 409 `TODAY_NOT_SELECTED` and nothing is carried (`checkSelection` → `todayNotSelected`), so it is never implied by selecting the earlier days. **D must be the company's today or later** (PR9 second review): a D before today answers 409 `DAY_OVER` ("24 Sep is over (today is 27 Sep): orders can only be brought forward to today or a later day. ...") and the preview of such a day lists nothing (`dayOver`), so the box is not shown when looking back at an earlier day; before, opening 24 Sep on 27 Sep offered every order of 17-23 Sep, ticked, and one click put them on a day whose loads had left. One transaction (60 s): `lockIntake` (confirm, late order and batch delete take it too), then the day lock (`lockPlanDay`, in date order) of every day it reads - today's too when its orders are listed: its loads are still moving, so a load of today dispatched meanwhile is seen, and one locked, loaded or dispatched after the carry is refused (`ORDERS_CARRIED`) - `createNextVersion` and `createInitialPlan` take it, so no new version of those days appears meanwhile - then `SELECT ... FOR UPDATE` on their live plans (in id order), checked to still be the live plans (409 `PLAN_BUSY` otherwise: nothing is read from a version that is not locked, so a load of it cannot be dispatched unseen; PR9 review), then everything is read again from exactly those plans. The lock order is intake → day → plan row. `checkSelection`: an order already carried is skipped (`ALREADY_CARRIED`, not an error), so running it twice, or twice at the same time, carries nothing new; an order no longer a candidate, blocked now, or with other open cases than shown answers **409 `CARRY_OVER_CHANGED`** (with `changed`) and nothing is carried. For each order it creates the copy on D (`carryCopyData`): same customer (so branch), depot D's, priority and `priorityFromFile` (**no automatic bump**), notes, and one line per open line with the same product, sales order, `weightFromMaster` and weight per case (kg, value and margin shared out by cases), `status VALIDATED`, no upload batch (deleting the original's file can never delete the copy), `carriedFromOrderId` = the original and `carriedFromDate` = the date first due (the first order's date when carried again). Late like a late order: `isLate` = after the cutoff of D or D already has a plan in use; `lateReason` "Brought forward from 26 Sep: not delivered on that day.". The copy's sales-order lines get their `IntakeLineKey` on D (the original keeps its own on its day): a file or late order for D with the same line is a duplicate, the copy is never a duplicate of its original. The original gets `carriedToOrderId`, `carriedAt` and `carriedById`. One audit row `ORDERS_CARRIED_OVER` (entity Order) lists every carry. Unique indexes on `carriedFromOrderId` and `carriedToOrderId` back the lock: an order has at most one copy. A timeout waiting for the intake lock answers 409 `INTAKE_BUSY`, for a plan row 409 `PLAN_BUSY`.
- **On the original's day** the original is no longer open, unserved or pending: `ordersInScopeWhere` leaves out an order with `carriedToOrderId`, so the day overview, the plan's pending orders, `pendingLateOrderIds`, the next re-plan of that day and the customer and product deactivation warnings (`open-orders.ts`) no longer count it, and the dashboard subtracts it from that day's orders and unserved, and its carried cases (the copy's) from that day's cases, so cost per case counts every case once (`CARRIED_OUT_OF_PLAN` in `lib/dashboard.ts`; the cases since the PR9 review). The Orders list (`GET /api/orders` and the upload page's Orders tab, `lib/orders-list.ts`) marks the original "Carried over to 28 Sep" and the copy "Carried over from 27 Sep"; a filter on an open status (UPLOADED, VALIDATED, ASSIGNED, UNSERVED) never returns a carried original (`status=CARRIED` lists them), and the tab's totals count its cases once (PR9 review). **Its plan versions stay exactly as they were** (no row is changed; the stored reconciliation still balances): the plan screen shows such an order as "Carried over to 28 Sep" (on its stop, in the unserved list instead of the reason, and a line under the reconciliation bar: history, not a problem), the Excel SUMMARY has a **CARRIED OVER** part. A stop is marked only on a load that never left (`carriedLoadShows`): the part of a split order on a dispatched or completed load was delivered, so that load, its driver sheet and its Excel load sheet show no mark and it counts no carried order (PR9 review; the unserved rest still says "Carried over to"). A load of that day that still holds such an order cannot be locked, loaded or dispatched ([3.8](#38-step-6-load-states), 409 `ORDERS_CARRIED`; for a load of today the words say "re-plan today" or "unlock it"); a load holding only such orders stays for the record (the plan screen's Re-plan is off when nothing else is open: `replanWork`): a PLANNED one needs nothing, a LOCKED or LOADING one was loaded, so its cases are unloaded before the later day's loads are picked and it is put back to Planned (`carriedLoadRemedy`, PR9 second review), and an optimize start whose open orders were carried meanwhile answers 409 `ORDERS_CHANGED`. Frozen loads of that day keep their carried orders in scope (history). A re-plan made after the carry reconciles such an order against the parts it still holds on those frozen loads (`carriedHeldCases` in `refreshPlanFacts`; the rest is the copy's, on its new day), so a split order with a dispatched part and a carried rest reconciles and the day's other loads can still be locked and dispatched (PR9 review: before, that version stayed "uploaded 100 != planned 60 + unserved 0" and every lock was refused 409 `NOT_RECONCILED`). The plan's summary still counts the order with all its cases (its carried rest as not delivered on that day), and the dashboard subtracts the carried cases. The batch delete refuses a file whose orders were carried (409 `BATCH_CARRIED`).
- **On day D** the copies are ordinary orders: with no plan yet, OPTIMIZE plans them with the others. While D's plan is being optimized (`OPTIMIZING`, also queued), that optimization was started without them: `bringForward` answers `replanNeeded` and `optimizing`, and the toast says RE-PLAN adds them once it finishes (PR9 second review: it said OPTIMIZE plans them); the day screen keeps the button off meanwhile. With a plan in use they are pending like late orders (`pending.carried`; `pendingLateOrderIds` counts them, so RE-PLAN runs with reason `LATE_ORDER` and plan continuity): the new version keeps the locked, loading and dispatched loads exactly as they are and plans the copies on the rest, or leaves them `LATE_ORDER_NO_CAPACITY`. They are marked everywhere a dispatcher or driver looks: the day screen's list of orders brought forward, "Carried over from 26 Sep" on the plan's stops and unserved rows, "CARRIED OVER from 26 Sep (not delivered that day)" on the driver sheet (PDF) and in the Notes of the Excel truck-load sheet, and the **CARRIED OVER** part of SUMMARY ("Brought forward from earlier days: N orders · M cases", per first-due day with the customers and loads).
- **Not in PR9** (possible owner settings later, [7.5](#75-open-questions-for-the-owner)): raising the priority of a carried order (it keeps its own), a window other than 7 days, and bringing orders forward by themselves (it is always the dispatcher's click).

**Which button sends which reason:**

- "Re-plan" on `PlanView` always sends `REOPTIMIZE`; it becomes `LATE_ORDER` when late orders are waiting.
- The late-order dialog's follow-up sends `LATE_ORDER`.
- The Step 3 RE-PLAN sends `LATE_ORDER` when pending late orders exist, else `REOPTIMIZE`.
- The API also accepts `MANUAL_ADJUSTMENT`, but no screen sends it.

Older versions stay readable at `/t/[slug]/dispatch/plan/[id]`. A version is superseded when its status is SUPERSEDED **or** `supersededAt` is set (`isSupersededRun` in `lib/dispatch/plan-status.ts`, PR3: before a race could write READY over a superseded version). On a superseded version every editing control is hidden, its driver sheets, workbook and WhatsApp messages say "do not use", the versions list shows it as SUPERSEDED, `currentPlan` never returns it, and the server refuses changes anyway (`lockOpenRun`, `applyScenario`, `startDispatchOptimize`, `replan`, choose-scenario, the legacy whole-run dispatch).

**When Re-plan is offered.** The plan screen's Re-plan is disabled while any action runs, while optimizing, and when there is nothing to plan: no PLANNED load, nothing unserved and no new order waiting (`PlanDetail.pendingOrders`, `nothingToReplan`). The day screen's OPTIMIZE / RE-PLAN is off with "Every order of this day is already on a locked, loading or dispatched load" when the day overview's `openOrders` is 0 (followed by "unlock it first" only when a LOCKED or LOADING load exists, from `plan.loadsByStatus`), and on again after a failed optimization. The day screen and the plan screen share one busy state (`onBusyChange` / `externalBusy` on `PlanView`, PR3 review): while "Lock all loads", a load change, Use instead or the plan's Re-plan runs, Step 3's OPTIMIZE / RE-PLAN is disabled, and while Step 3's request runs, the plan's actions are disabled, so one dispatcher cannot re-plan in the middle of their own "Lock all". Each side stays busy until the day screen shows the result of its action, not only until its request answers: the plan's actions run through `runPlanAction` (`plan-actions.ts`) and await the day's reload (`onChanged` returns it), and OPTIMIZE / RE-PLAN keeps its flag until its own reload; both give the busy state back in a `finally`, so an error never leaves the buttons disabled. After a late order is saved (`afterLateOrderSaved`), "Re-plan now?" re-plans without reloading the day first (the re-plan reloads it when it ends, also after a refusal or a declined location / weight question); "no", or a new customer without a location, reloads the day. Second review of PR3: the day was reloaded before the re-plan, which replaced the plan screen before the re-plan took the busy state, so Step 3's RE-PLAN stayed clickable while that re-plan ran (a second click: 409 `SUPERSEDED`).

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    autonumber
    actor D as Dispatcher
    participant UI as plan-view.tsx
    participant LO as POST /api/dispatch/late-order
    participant RP as POST /api/runs/ID/replan
    participant SO as start-optimize.replan
    participant PS as plan-service.ts
    participant DB as Postgres via Prisma
    D->>UI: Late order dialog - customer, lines, priority, reason
    UI->>LO: record the order
    LO->>DB: one transaction - find or create Customer and Products, create Order VALIDATED with isLate
    LO->>DB: after commit - AuditLog LATE_ORDER_RECORDED
    D->>UI: Re-plan now
    UI->>RP: reason LATE_ORDER, expect date and depot
    RP->>SO: replan
    SO->>PS: day of the screen? superseded? legacy? (409 DAY_MISMATCH, SUPERSEDED, LEGACY_PLAN)
    alt parent has no applied scenario
        SO->>SO: startDispatchOptimize on the same version
    else parent is applied
        SO->>DB: optimizing or a job running? (409)
        SO->>PS: buildDispatchRequest on the parent as a probe (weights in memory only)
        Note over SO,PS: refused before any version exists - 409 LOCATION_REQUIRED or WEIGHT_REQUIRED, 409 NOTHING_TO_PLAN, 400 no active truck
        SO->>PS: pendingLateOrderIds - a REOPTIMIZE with late orders waiting becomes LATE_ORDER
        SO->>SO: reserve the solve admission (429 or 503 leaves no version)
        SO->>PS: createNextVersion
        PS->>DB: day lock, then parent FOR UPDATE (refused if superseded, optimizing or a job runs)
        PS->>DB: create child N+1 with parentRunId and reason, copy EVERY PlanLoad with its RouteAssignments (carriedFromLoadId)
        PS->>DB: copy the chosen ScenarioResult with its UnservedOrders, summary and reconciliation - child READY, or DISPATCHED when every load is out
        PS->>DB: parent SUPERSEDED with supersededAt, AuditLog PLAN_VERSION_CREATED
        SO->>SO: startDispatchOptimize on the child (freshVersion, with the reserved ticket)
        Note over SO,PS: the child request keeps frozen trips and sends previous_truck_id only when usesPlanContinuity
    end
    SO-->>UI: 202 with the child runId, version and effective reason
    Note over UI,DB: the job then runs as in the optimize diagram, on the child - on success it saves the weights and replaces the copied PLANNED loads and option, on failure the child stays FAILED holding the copied plan
```

### 3.12 Settings that change the plan

Since stabilization PR5 (review F21) the Settings page shows **only the settings the daily dispatch planner uses**, each with its unit and allowed range, and a read-only **Effective planner values** panel that says what the next optimization uses and where each value comes from (a setting, a fixed planner rule, or an operations setting). The page is `apps/web/app/t/[slug]/settings/settings-form.tsx`; it saves through `PATCH /api/tenant/config` (`apps/web/app/api/tenant/config/route.ts`, TENANT_ADMIN). Changes apply to the next OPTIMIZE or re-plan; plans already made keep what they were made with (`ScenarioDetails.inputs`, PR4).

- **One mapping.** `dispatchConfigFromTenant` (`lib/dispatch/planner-config.ts`) turns the settings and the company country into the optimizer's config. `buildDispatchRequest` and the panel (`effectivePlannerValues`) both use it.
- **Bounds.** Every field is validated against `lib/planner-bounds.ts`, a copy of `packages/shared-types/src/planner-bounds.json`; each range lies inside the optimizer's Pydantic contract (`apps/solver/dispatch_models.py`). Tests check both sides against the JSON (`tests/lib/tenant-settings.spec.ts`, `apps/solver/tests/test_dispatch.py`). Overtime after must be at most the shift maximum, checked on the merged settings when a save changes the overtime threshold or the shift maximum (`overtimeSaveProblem`, the route and the form alike). A stored threshold after a lowered shift maximum is a planner warning and an amber note on the page; it no longer blocks saving the company name or another field (PR5 review).
- **Strict.** `tenantConfigSchema` is strict: the old controls that changed nothing (solver time limit, objective weights, cost and penalty defaults, "return to depot", "label estimated distances", the Mapbox provider) answer 400. Their columns stay in the database, marked `@deprecated` in `schema.prisma`; they are dropped in a later expand-then-contract release.
- **No silent overwrite.** The page sends only the fields it changed, with the values it showed (`expect`). The route locks the `TenantConfig` row and answers **409 `SETTINGS_CHANGED`** (naming the fields) when another admin or tab saved a different value meanwhile; nothing is saved. The audit row `UPDATE TenantConfig` lists only the changed fields, before and after.
- **Out-of-range stored values** (a direct database edit) refuse the optimize with **409 `SETTINGS_OUT_OF_RANGE`** naming the setting, instead of a solver 422.

| Settings card | `TenantConfig` field (label) |
|---|---|
| Company | `Tenant.name`, `country` (**pick-list** since PR5: Oman, United Arab Emirates, Saudi Arabia, Qatar, Bahrain, Kuwait, Other; `lib/countries.ts`; it decides road routing: outside Oman + UAE plans use straight-line estimates unless the company has its own routing server), `currency`, `primaryUnit` |
| Daily dispatch: timing | `shiftStartMin` (First departure; a plan made on the delivery day starts from now + turnaround when later, PR8), `driverShiftMaxMinutes` (Driver shift maximum), `reloadMinutes` (Turnaround between loads; since PR8 also the preparation time of a same-day plan, [3.11](#311-step-9-late-orders-and-plan-versions)), `loadingMinPerCase` (on a plan made on the delivery day it counts from now for every truck, PR8 review), `serviceMinPerCase`, `defaultServiceTimeMin` (Default service time), `maxTripsPerTruck`, `splitDeliveries`, `planningCutoffMin` (Planning cutoff, used by the order intake), `dateOrder` (dates in order files, used by the order intake) |
| Daily dispatch: costs | `driverCostPerHour` (per hour of the **whole truck day**, [4.10](#410-extraction-and-reported-costs)), `overtimeAfterMin`, `overtimeCostPerHour` (on top of the driver cost), `fuelPricePerLitre`, `prefWindowPenaltyPerMin` |
| Distances | `distanceProvider` (road distances OSRM, or straight-line estimates), `roadTimeFactor` (truck vs car, road legs only), `distanceMultiplier` and `avgSpeedKmh` (estimates) |
| Effective planner values (read-only) | everything above, plus the operations settings `timezone`, `osrmUrl` (shown as "company server set" or not, never editable: review L11), `serviceAreaJson`, and the planner rules (strict priorities, early-arrival preference, plan continuity, automatic search time - PR7: "5 s up to 25 stops, 20 s up to 120, rising steadily to 50 s at 150 and 150 s at 200, 150 s up to 350, 240 s above", from the schedule in `planner-bounds.json` that the solver tests check, `searchTimeText()` in `lib/dispatch/planner-config.ts` - 600 stops, 90 s road routing) |
| Customer type defaults (read-only) | `CustomerTypeProfile`: default priority, unloading minutes, hard and preferred hours per customer type. No editor yet (backlog) |

**Default service time (PR5).** An unloading time a planner confirmed (customer screen, or an imported column) wins; otherwise the customer type's; otherwise the company default from Settings. Before PR5 an unconfirmed customer kept its column default (10 min) and the Settings default changed nothing for normal customers. A new customer created with an explicit unloading time counts as confirmed. Customer imports before PR2 stored the file's time without confirming it; migration `20260928090100_keep_imported_service_times` marks those times confirmed (only where the type has no time of its own and the value is not the 10 min column default; one audit row each), so they are not replaced by the Settings default. The customer page shows the unloading time in use and where it comes from (`describeServiceTime`): an amber note when a stored time someone entered is not used (never confirmed); nothing for the 10 min column default under a customer type's own time (customers created by an order upload, a late order or a form without a time hold it although nobody entered it; the type's time applied before and after PR5); a neutral line for a stored 10 min under the Settings default (it may be an earlier import's real 10 min: pre-deploy check 11 in [5.12](#512-how-to-verify-a-deploy) asks to confirm those). One constant, `CUSTOMER_SERVICE_COLUMN_DEFAULT`, is kept equal to the schema default and the migration's rule by a unit test.

Other inputs that change the plan:

- **Per truck** (Trucks page, `truckSchema`): capacity in cases and kg, fixed daily cost, cost per km, and since PR5 **cost per load** (`tripCost`), **km per litre**, **max loads per day** and **availability** (from / until, HH:MM). A new truck starts at 0 costs (the invented 20 OMR / 0.15 per km defaults are gone). "Until 00:00" (or 24:00) is midnight, stored as 1440; since the audit PR "Intake and master data" (F25, `lib/dispatch/time-input.ts`) the edit form shows 1440 as 00:00 and a field left as loaded saves its stored minutes, so a truck available until midnight saves again when another field changes (it showed "00:00 +1", which the form's own save refused).
- **Per depot** (Depots page, `depotSchema`): since PR5 **opening and closing hours** (`openMin` / `closeMin`).
- **Per customer type:** `CustomerTypeProfile` (read-only table on Settings; seeded).
- `priorityWeightsJson` (inert while priorities are strict) and `orderColumnMapJson` can only be set in the database.

### 3.13 Audit log

The page is `apps/web/app/t/[slug]/audit/page.tsx` with `audit-client.tsx`, limited to TENANT_ADMIN. It filters through `GET /api/audit` (`apps/web/app/api/audit/route.ts`), TENANT_ADMIN since PR1. Since PR1 `audit()` strips `accessPinHash`, `passwordHash`, `sessionToken` and `tokenHash` from before/after JSON, and the API and page strip them on read too.

**One catalog (PR5, review F23).** `lib/audit-catalog.ts` lists every action and entity with a label (`AUDIT_ACTIONS`, `AUDIT_ENTITIES`; the `LOAD_*` events are derived from the load statuses; events no longer written - `LOGOUT`, the retired driver app's `DRIVER_LOGIN` and `DELIVERY_PROOF_CREATED`, entities `RunJob` and `DriverShift` - are kept and marked old). Every writer goes through `audit()` (the plan transactions pass their `tx`), whose `action` and `entity` are typed with the catalog, so an event cannot be written without being findable. The page's filter lists come from it.

**API filters.** `action`, `entity`, `userId`, `from`, `to` (YYYY-MM-DD) and `limit` (1-1000, default 200; the page asks for 500) are zod-parsed; an unknown action, entity or parameter answers **400 `INVALID_FILTER`** with the allowed names (before PR5 an unknown action was dropped and the answer was every row). `from` / `to` are days in the company timezone (Asia/Muscat): `from=2026-09-25` starts at 2026-09-24T20:00Z. The page shows an error instead of "0 rows" when a query fails, and says when only the newest rows are shown.

| Action | Entity | Written by |
|---|---|---|
| `CREATE` | UploadBatch | `POST /api/orders/upload` |
| `UPDATE` | UploadBatch | confirm route |
| `DELETE` | UploadBatch | `DELETE /api/orders/:batchId` (legacy upload page), in the same transaction as the delete |
| `ORDER_WEIGHTS_RESOLVED` | RunPlan | `applyWeightChanges`, in the job's transaction that saves the plan made with those weights (never for a re-plan probe, never for a failed or stale optimization; PR3 review): each line and order kg before and after, with the product |
| `CUSTOMER_LOCATION_SET` | Customer | `PUT /api/customers/:id/location` (since audit PR A5 with the confidence and how the point was checked: `HAND_PIN`, `READING` or `SAVED_POINT`) |
| `UPDATE` | Customer | `PATCH /api/customers/:id` |
| `CREATE` | RunPlan | `createInitialPlan` |
| `OPTIMIZE_STARTED` | RunPlan | `startDispatchOptimize` |
| `OPTIMIZE_SUCCEEDED`, `OPTIMIZE_FAILED` | RunPlan | `dispatch-job.ts`; `reapStuckJobs` for stuck jobs (reason `STUCK`). Since audit PR4 always in the transaction that fails the job and its plan; `OPTIMIZE_FAILED` with `afterJson.reason = 'STUCK_PLAN'` (and `repairedBy`: `JANITOR`, `OPTIMIZE` or `REPLAN`) is the repair of a plan left OPTIMIZING behind an ended job. Since PR4 each scenario in `OPTIMIZE_SUCCEEDED` carries `feasibility` (status, timing, number of violations, codes), and the job message says when the recommended timetable is not verified |
| `SCENARIO_CHOSEN` | RunPlan | `applyScenario` (every optimize and every "Use instead"); `driverChanges` lists the driver notes (truck, trip, from, to, reason: `CLASH`, `INACTIVE` or `TRIP_GONE`) |
| `PLAN_VERSION_CREATED` | RunPlan | `createNextVersion` |
| `PLAN_RESET` | RunPlan | (Audit PR4, F09, owner decision 17) **Reset stuck plan** by a SUPERVISOR or above (`resetStuckPlan`): before (OPTIMIZING, the current job and its status), after (FAILED, whether the lost job was failed, the kind: `JOB_ENDED`, `NO_JOB` or `JOB_LOST`, the optional note) |
| `UPDATE` | User | `PATCH /api/users/:id` (role, active); since audit PR4 (F11) in the transaction that locks the company row and checks that an active admin remains |
| `LOAD_LOCKED`, `LOAD_PLANNED`, `LOAD_LOADING`, `LOAD_DISPATCHED`, `LOAD_COMPLETED` | PlanLoad | `changeStatusTx` (`loadStatusAction`). A gated move (PR4) records `timing` (status, ok, gate, and the overridden violations under `FEASIBILITY_GATE=warn`) |
| `LOAD_DRIVER_SET` | PlanLoad | `setDriverTx` (the load is also marked: `driverSetById`, `driverSetAt`); **Keep** writes one with `afterJson.kept: true` and `beforeJson.byHand: false` |
| `LATE_ORDER_RECORDED` | Order | `POST /api/dispatch/late-order` |
| `ORDERS_CARRIED_OVER` | Order | `bringForward` (PR9, `POST /api/dispatch/carry-over`), in its transaction: the day, the depot, and per order the original, the copy, the customer, the original's date, the cases and whether it was a part |
| `UPDATE` | TenantConfig | `PATCH /api/tenant/config` (PR5: only the changed fields) |
| `LOGIN` | User | NextAuth `signIn` event (`lib/auth.ts`) |
| `LOGIN_THROTTLED` | User | `verifyCredentials` at 20 failed sign-ins for the account in an hour (PR1) |
| `PASSWORD_RESET_BY_ADMIN` | User | `POST /api/users/:id/reset-password` (PR1) |
| `CROSS_TENANT_VIEW` | Tenant | `getCurrentTenant`, a platform admin viewing this tenant (PR1) |
| `PLATFORM_ADMIN_GRANTED`, `PLATFORM_ADMIN_REVOKED` | User | `prisma/grant-platform-admin.ts` (PR1) |
| `SECURITY_CLEANUP` | Tenant | migration `20260926090000_retire_driver_app_scrub_secrets` (PR1) |
| `DEPOT_BACKFILL` | Tenant | migration `20260930120000_orders_always_have_depot` (audit PR A5), one row per company that had orders or files without a depot: how many got a depot from their file, their plans, the company's only depot or the history-only depot, the conflicts, and the history-only depot's code ([5.5](#55-database-migrations)) |

### 3.14 Status values and transitions

**`RunPlan.status` (`RunStatus`).**

| Value | Set by | Next |
|---|---|---|
| DRAFT | schema default: `createInitialPlan` (version 1, under the day lock), legacy `POST /api/runs` (same helper) | OPTIMIZING |
| OPTIMIZING | `startDispatchOptimize`, in the transaction that creates the new `RunJob` | READY, DISPATCHED, FAILED |
| READY | `applyScenario` (job or Use instead) and `changeStatusTx` while not every load is out - `changeStatusTx` only on a version with an applied plan; `createNextVersion` for the child (copy of an applied plan) | DISPATCHED, OPTIMIZING (a re-plan child's first job), SUPERSEDED |
| FAILED | `failJob` in `dispatch-job.ts` and `reapStuckJobs`, only from OPTIMIZING with the failing job as `currentJobId`; (audit PR4) `repairEndedJobPlan` for a plan OPTIMIZING behind an ended job, and `resetStuckPlan` (Reset stuck plan) | OPTIMIZING only without an applied plan (a failed re-plan still holding the copied plan answers 409 `NEW_VERSION_REQUIRED`: re-plan instead), READY (a load change on a FAILED version with an applied plan), SUPERSEDED |
| DISPATCHED | `changeStatusTx` or `applyScenario` when every load is DISPATCHED or COMPLETED (sets `finalizedAt`); `createNextVersion` for a child whose loads are all out; legacy `POST /api/runs/:id/dispatch` for runs without loads | SUPERSEDED (a re-plan is not blocked), OPTIMIZING (a re-plan child's first job); legacy `/unlock` back to READY for runs without loads |
| SUPERSEDED | `createNextVersion` on the parent (sets `supersededAt`) | final, read-only. `supersededAt` set means superseded whatever the status (PR3) |
| ARCHIVED | never written by any code; only excluded by `currentPlan` | none |

Every writer of `RunPlan.status` holds the plan's row lock (PR3, review F07), taken by `lockRunForWrite` in `lib/dispatch/plan-locks.ts` after the day lock where there is one. Lock order everywhere: the day (advisory lock), then the `RunPlan` row, then its `PlanLoad` rows; the optimize start takes the intake lock before the row. No writer can overwrite a newer state: `applyScenario` writes conditionally (never over SUPERSEDED), `failJob` and the janitor only touch their own job's OPTIMIZING version, and a stale job result is not applied.

**`PlanLoad.status` (`LoadStatus`):** see [3.8](#38-step-6-load-states). Created as PLANNED by `applyScenario`, or copied with its current status by `createNextVersion` (every load, PLANNED included, since PR3).

**`Order.status` (`OrderStatus`).**

| Value | Set by |
|---|---|
| VALIDATED | `confirmIntake`, late-order route |
| ASSIGNED | `applyScenario` when at least one part is on a truck |
| UNSERVED | `applyScenario` when nothing of it is planned |
| DISPATCHED | `changeStatusTx` when every part is on DISPATCHED or COMPLETED loads and no part is unserved (a split order with an unserved rest stays ASSIGNED) |
| UPLOADED, DELIVERED, FAILED | not written by the dispatch flow (UPLOADED is only the schema default; DELIVERED and FAILED are never written in `apps/web`) |

An order brought forward to a later day (PR9) keeps its status (the history of its day); `carriedToOrderId` set is what makes it no longer open there. Its copy starts `VALIDATED`. A load of its day that still holds it cannot move forward (409 `ORDERS_CARRIED`, [3.8](#38-step-6-load-states)); a PLANNED one holding nothing else stays as it is, while a LOCKED or LOADING one holding nothing else was loaded (NMWC loads at night): its cases are unloaded back to stock before the later day's loads are picked and it is put back to Planned (PR9 second review; the answer, the plan badge and Step 3 say so). The same holds for a load of today whose order of today was brought forward to tomorrow in the evening; its answer says re-plan today, or unlock it (a LOADING one goes Back to locked first).

`applyScenario` never moves an order that is already DISPATCHED or DELIVERED.

**`UploadBatch.status`:** PARSED (uploaded with errors) or VALIDATED (no errors), then CONFIRMED, or DELETED (`DELETE /api/orders/:batchId`, which also deletes its orders, allowed only while none of them is in a plan option). A VALIDATED batch older than 24 h cannot be confirmed (`STALE_VALIDATION`); it stays VALIDATED. REJECTED is checked for but never written.

**`RunJob.status`:** QUEUED (also while waiting for a solver slot, message "Waiting: N optimization(s) ahead"), RUNNING, then SUCCEEDED or FAILED (`failJob`; `reapStuckJobs` with `errorJson.reason = 'STUCK'`; a result for a version that moved on with `errorJson.reason = 'STALE_RESULT'`; (audit PR4) Reset stuck plan with `errorJson.reason = 'RESET'`). CANCELLED is never written. **Invariant (audit PR4, F09):** a plan is OPTIMIZING only while its current job is QUEUED or RUNNING.

### 3.15 Data model used by the dispatch flow

```mermaid
erDiagram
    Tenant ||--|| TenantConfig : settings
    Tenant ||--o{ CustomerTypeProfile : "type defaults"
    Depot ||--o{ Truck : bases
    Driver |o--o{ Truck : "default driver"
    Customer ||--o{ Order : orders
    UploadBatch |o--o{ Order : "created by"
    Order ||--|{ OrderLine : lines
    Product ||--o{ OrderLine : sku
    Depot ||--o{ RunPlan : "plan per date"
    RunPlan |o--o{ RunPlan : "parent version"
    RunPlan ||--o{ RunJob : attempts
    RunPlan ||--o{ ScenarioResult : options
    ScenarioResult ||--o{ UnservedOrder : unserved
    Order ||--o{ UnservedOrder : "left out"
    RunPlan ||--o{ PlanLoad : loads
    Truck ||--o{ PlanLoad : trips
    Driver |o--o{ PlanLoad : drives
    PlanLoad ||--o{ RouteAssignment : stops
    Order ||--o{ RouteAssignment : "planned as"
```

| Model | Role in the flow | Key fields and relations |
|---|---|---|
| `Tenant` | owner of everything | `country` (service area, OSRM coverage, WhatsApp country code), `currency`, `name` |
| `TenantConfig` | planning settings, one per tenant | see [3.12](#312-settings-that-change-the-plan) |
| `CustomerTypeProfile` | defaults per `CustomerType` | `defaultPriority`, `serviceTimeMin`, hard and preferred windows; unique (`tenantId`, `customerType`) |
| `Depot` | start and end of every load | `lat`, `lng`, `openMin`, `closeMin`; unique (`tenantId`, `code`); `historyOnly` (audit PR A5: the depot `NO-DEPOT` "No depot (kept for history)" that migration `20260930120000_orders_always_have_depot` made for orders and files that had no depot; always inactive, never in a picker, never gets a truck or region; the database refuses it active too, CHECK `Depot_historyOnly_not_active`, A5 fifth review) |
| `Truck` | vehicle at a depot | `capacityCases`, `capacityWeightKg`, `fixedCostPerDay`, `tripCost`, `costPerKm`, `kmPerLitre`, `maxTripsPerDay`, `availableFromMin`, `availableToMin`, `defaultDriverId`, `active` |
| `Driver` | person on a load | `name`, `phone` (WhatsApp), `active`; `loads` |
| `Customer` | delivery location (code + branch) | unique (`tenantId`, `code`, `branchKey`); `lat`, `lng`, `priority` + `priorityConfirmed`, `avgServiceTimeMin` + `serviceTimeConfirmed`, `customerType`, hard and preferred windows, `locationInput`, `locationSource`, `locationVerified`, `locationVerifiedById`, `locationVerifiedAt`, `createdFromUpload`, `address` and `accessNotes` (driver sheet) |
| `Product` | SKU | unique (`tenantId`, `code`); `weightPerCaseKg` (0 = unknown), `createdFromUpload` |
| `UploadBatch` | one uploaded file | `status`, `validationJson` (full validation including resolved lines), `fileHash`, `depotId` (required since audit PR A5, FK Depot NO ACTION), `deliveryDate`, `isLate`, `lateReason` |
| `Order` | one customer branch for one delivery date (or one late-order entry, or one order brought forward) | `customerId`, `depotId` (required since audit PR A5: every order has a depot; FK Depot NO ACTION), `deliveryDate`, `totalCases`, `totalWeightKg`, `priority`, `priorityFromFile`, `status`, `isLate`, `lateReason`, `lateRecordedById`, `salesValue`, `marginValue`, `notes`, `uploadBatchId`; (PR9, migration `20260929090000_order_carry_over`) on a copy `carriedFromOrderId` (unique, FK Order, NO ACTION) and `carriedFromDate` (the date first due); on the original `carriedToOrderId` (unique, FK Order, NO ACTION), `carriedAt`, `carriedById` (FK User, SET NULL) |
| `OrderLine` | one SKU line | `productId`, `cases`, `salesOrderNo`, `weightKg` (0 = unknown), `weightFromMaster` (no file weight: the kg is cases × the product's case weight and follows it; set again at optimize when that weight is entered or corrected), `salesValue`, `marginValue`, `sourceRow`, `notes`; cascades with its order |
| `IntakeLineKey` | identity of a confirmed sales-order line | unique (`tenantId`, `deliveryDate`, `salesOrderNorm` (trimmed, upper-case), `customerId`, `productId`); `orderLineId` unique, cascades with the line; `uploadBatchId` (null for a late order). Written by `confirmIntake` and the late-order route; backfilled for existing lines by migration `20260926100000_intake_line_keys` |
| `RunPlan` | one version of the plan for (`depotId`, `runDate`) | `version`, `parentRunId` (self relation "PlanVersions"), `reason`, `reasonNote`, `status`, `chosenScenarioId` (plain id, no foreign key), `currentJobId`, `supersededAt`, `finalizedAt`, `summaryJson`, `reconciliationJson`, `changeSummaryJson`, `feasibilityJson` (PR4: the timetable check per truck-day, [3.8](#38-step-6-load-states)), `totalOrders`, `unservedCount` |
| `RunJob` | one optimize attempt | unique (`runId`, `attemptNo`); `status`, `progressPct`, `message`, `requestJson`, `responseJson`, `errorJson`, timestamps |
| `ScenarioResult` | one solver option | `name` (RECOMMENDED, MIN_TRUCKS, MIN_DISTANCE), totals, `detailsJson` (`ScenarioDetails` including `scope`, the solver's `feasibility` report and, since PR4, `inputs`: what the optimization was computed with, [`lib/dispatch/snapshots.ts`](#22-the-web-app)) |
| `UnservedOrder` | an order (or part) left out of one option | `scenarioId`, `orderId`, `reasonCode`, `reasonMessage`, `portionCases`, `portionWeightKg`, `portionLinesJson` |
| `PlanLoad` | one physical truck trip | unique (`runId`, `truckId`, `loadNo`); `status`, `driverId`, `departMin`, `returnMin`, `distanceKm`, `durationMin`, `cases`, `weightKg`, `utilizationPct`, `fuelLitres`, `fuelCost`, `operatingCost`, `returnLegKm`, `distanceIsEstimated`, `truckSnapshotJson` (PR4: the truck code, capacities, costs, availability and the planning rules the load was timed with; null before PR4), `carriedFromLoadId`, `statusChangedAt`, `statusChangedById`, `driverSetById` (FK User, SET NULL) and `driverSetAt`: who chose the driver by hand, and when (null: RouteIQ filled it in) |
| `RouteAssignment` | one order (or split portion) at one stop of one load | unique (`runId`, `truckId`, `loadNo`, `sequenceInTruck`, `orderInStop`); `loadId`, `etaMin`, `serviceStartMin`, `departureMin`, `waitMin`, `cumulativeKm`, `hardWindowOk`, `prefWindowOk`, `portionCases`, `portionWeightKg` (the parts of one split stop add up exactly to the kg the optimizer was sent, PR4 review), `portionLinesJson` (`[{ lineId, cases, kgPerCase }]`; `kgPerCase`, the case weight the part was planned with, since the PR4 review), `stopSnapshotJson` (PR4: the pin, hours, service time and priority sent to the optimizer, plus the customer's code, name, type, address and access notes when applied - the notes for the record only, sheets print today's; null before PR4) |
| `AuditLog` | who did what | `action`, `entity`, `entityId`, `beforeJson`, `afterJson`, `ip` |

**Delete behaviour.** Deleting a `RunPlan` cascades to its jobs, scenarios, loads and assignments. `RouteAssignment.orderId` is `ON DELETE RESTRICT` and `UnservedOrder.orderId` is `ON DELETE NO ACTION` (since migration `20260926100100_unserved_order_fk_no_action`; it was CASCADE), so an order that is on any plan option, planned or unserved, cannot be deleted. Deleting a tenant therefore removes its plan data first (as `cleanupTenant` in the integration helpers does). An order brought forward and its copy (PR9) point at each other `ON DELETE NO ACTION`: neither can be deleted from under the other (one statement deleting both, as a tenant delete does, is fine). Since migration `20260930093000_master_data_no_orphans` (audit F03 / F20) `Order.depotId`, `UploadBatch.depotId`, `PlanLoad.driverId` and `Truck.defaultDriverId` are `ON DELETE NO ACTION` too (they were SET NULL): a depot with orders or order files, and a driver on a load or set as a truck's default, cannot be deleted - the app deactivates them instead. `Truck.depotId` and `RunPlan.depotId` were already RESTRICT; `Region.depotId` stays SET NULL (the app deactivates a depot with regions anyway).

**Legacy-only models** (neither read nor written by the dispatch flow): `ManualBaseline` and `ManualBaselineAssignment` (baseline tab of the old run page); `DriverShift`, `TruckLocation` and `DeliveryProof` (the driver PWA, retired in PR1; data kept); `Region` (master-data page only; `Customer.regionId`). `User` and `PasswordResetToken` belong to authentication.

**Legacy-only fields and values.**

- `RunPlan.optimizationMode`: copied into child versions, never sent to the solver.
- `RouteAssignment.lockedByUserId` and `manualOverrideReason`: per-stop locks of the old flow. `plannedArrivalMin`, `plannedDistanceFromPrevKm` and `plannedLoadCases` are still filled.
- `Order.totalServiceTimeMin`, `totalVolumeL` and `paymentCollectionAmount`: written or defaulted, not read by the planner.
- `Truck.capacityVolumeL` and `palletCapacity` (not sent to the solver); `Customer.paymentType`.
- Enum values the dispatch flow never writes: `RunStatus.ARCHIVED`; `OrderStatus` UPLOADED, DELIVERED and FAILED; `UploadBatchStatus.REJECTED`; `RunJobStatus.CANCELLED`; the `OptimizationMode` enum; and the older `UnservedReasonCode` values (`EXCEEDS_TRUCK_CAPACITY`, `NO_AVAILABLE_TRUCK`, `SHIFT_TIME_LIMIT`, `INFEASIBLE_ROUTE`). For old plans, `REASON_TEXT` in `app/t/[slug]/dispatch/client-api.ts` labels `EXCEEDS_TRUCK_CAPACITY`, `SHIFT_TIME_LIMIT`, `INFEASIBLE_ROUTE` and `NO_AVAILABLE_TRUCK`. `INVALID_CUSTOMER` is written again since the stabilization release, for open orders of a deactivated customer ("Customer deactivated"). (The solver does emit `NO_AVAILABLE_TRUCK`; see [4.12](#412-unserved-reason-codes-and-messages).)

### 3.16 API route map for the dispatch flow

| Method | Path | Role | Logic | Called from |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/api/dispatch/day` | any | `getDayOverview` | `DispatchClient.refresh` |
| POST | `/api/orders/upload` | PLANNER, rate limited | `parseUploadIsolated` (runs `parseUpload` in the parser process), `validateIntake`, `fileHash` | `DispatchClient.upload`; legacy `upload-dropzone.tsx` |
| POST | `/api/orders/:batchId/confirm` | PLANNER | `lockIntake`, `revalidateIntake`, `confirmIntake` | `DispatchClient.confirmBatch`; legacy `validation-report.tsx` |
| DELETE | `/api/orders/:batchId` | PLANNER | refused once any order is in a plan option | legacy `batches-table.tsx` |
| POST | `/api/locations/parse` | PLANNER, rate limited | `resolveLocationInput` | `LocationDialog` "Read" |
| PUT | `/api/customers/:id/location` | PLANNER | `checkManualLocation` (a reading read again, no network: 422 `PIN_REQUIRED` / `LOCATION_MISMATCH`), the saved-point and hand-pin checks, `resolveLocationInput` for `{ input }` alone, `coordStatus` (audit PR A5) | `LocationDialog` "Save location" (Daily dispatch and the customer page's Set location) |
| PATCH | `/api/customers/:id` | PLANNER | customer update: only the fields sent, each confirming only itself; `avgServiceTimeMin: null` = back to the default, not confirmed; times and minutes JSON numbers only (audit F07); coordinates refused, 400 `USE_SET_LOCATION` (audit PR A5) | `CustomerDialog` (only the changed fields) |
| POST | `/api/dispatch/plan` | PLANNER, solve admission | `getOrCreatePlan` (`createInitialPlan` under the day lock), `startDispatchOptimize`; optional `expect` (409 `DAY_MISMATCH`) | `DispatchClient.optimize` when no plan is applied |
| POST | `/api/runs/:id/replan` | PLANNER, solve admission | `replan` (refusals first: 409 `NOTHING_TO_PLAN`, 400 no truck, 409 job running, 429 / 503), `createNextVersion` (copy-forward), `startDispatchOptimize`; optional `expect` (409 `DAY_MISMATCH`) | `DispatchClient.optimize` when a plan is applied; `PlanView` Re-plan and late-order follow-up |
| POST | `/api/runs/:id/optimize` | PLANNER, solve admission | `startDispatchOptimize` | legacy `run-detail.tsx` only |
| POST | `/api/runs/:id/reset-stuck` | SUPERVISOR (audit PR4) | `resetStuckPlan` (plan row lock; 409 `NOT_STUCK` / `JOB_RUNNING` / `JOB_STARTING`; audit `PLAN_RESET`) | `PlanView` **Reset stuck plan** (shown under *Optimizing…* when `PlanDetail.stuck` is resettable) |
| GET | `/api/runs/:id/plan` | any | `getPlanDetail` (one REPEATABLE READ snapshot since audit PR4, F21) | `PlanView` |
| POST | `/api/runs/:id/choose-scenario` | PLANNER | `chooseScenario` (row lock, then `applyScenario`; `NO_SOLUTION` refused) | `PlanView` "Use instead"; legacy `run-detail.tsx` |
| PATCH | `/api/runs/:id/loads/:loadId` | PLANNER (DISPATCHED and COMPLETED need SUPERVISOR) | `updateLoad` (row lock, 5 s lock timeout: 409 `PLAN_BUSY`) | `PlanView` status buttons, "Lock all loads", driver picker |
| GET | `/api/drivers` | any | driver list without PIN hashes | `PlanView` driver picker |
| GET | `/api/runs/:id/load-geometry` | any | `resolveLoadGeometries` → `callRouteGeometry` per load (4 at a time, 20 s deadline, cached road shapes); `pointsKey` per row | `PlanMap` (reloads the plan through `PlanView`'s `load` when the answer is stale) |
| GET | `/api/runs/:id/export/excel` | any | `isDispatchPlan`, then `getPlanDetail`, `buildDispatchWorkbook` (also for a plan without loads, audit F16); legacy runs get the route sheet | `PlanView` "Export Excel" |
| GET | `/api/runs/:id/export/pdf` | any | `isDispatchPlan`, then `getPlanDetail`, `driverPackModel`, `renderDriverPackPdf`; a dispatch plan without loads 404 `NO_LOADS`; legacy runs get the route sheet | `PlanView` "Driver sheets (PDF)" and per-load "PDF" |
| POST | `/api/dispatch/late-order` | PLANNER | order, lines and stubs in one transaction | `LateOrderDialog` |
| GET | `/api/dispatch/carry-over?date&depotId` | any | `carryOverPreview` (PR9): orders of the 7 days before (never after the company's today; today's marked `ofToday` and counted apart in `todayOrders` / `todayCases`; `from`, `to` and `today` in the answer) not delivered, why, and the ones that cannot be carried; nothing for a day that is over (`dayOver`); 400 for a bad date or another tenant's depot | `CarryOverPanel` (Step 1) |
| POST | `/api/dispatch/carry-over` | PLANNER | `bringForward` (PR9): `{ date, depotId, selected: [{ orderId, cases, today? }] }` (zod, strict; `cases` = the open cases shown; `today: true` = ticked in the Today group); intake lock, day locks and plan row locks (409 `PLAN_BUSY` when a day's live plan changed while it was locked), copies and marks in one transaction; 201 with what was carried (200 when nothing new; `replanNeeded`, and `optimizing` while D's plan is being optimized), 409 `CARRY_OVER_CHANGED` / `INTAKE_BUSY` / `PLAN_BUSY` / `DAY_OVER` (D before the company's today) / `TODAY_NOT_SELECTED` (an order of today sent without `today: true`: nothing carried), 400 for another tenant's depot; another tenant's order ids are "changed" (409), never carried | `CarryOverPanel` "Bring forward to <day>" |
| GET | `/api/runs/:id/status` | any | `RunPlan` and current `RunJob` | legacy `run-detail.tsx` polling |
| GET | `/api/runs/:id/jobs/:jobId/debug` | any | `RunJob` request, response and error JSON as a download | legacy `run-detail.tsx` |
| GET, PATCH | `/api/tenant/config` | GET any, PATCH TENANT_ADMIN | `TenantConfig` read and update | Settings page |
| GET, POST | `/api/cron/janitor` | `X-Janitor-Token` header | `reapStuckJobs`, `reapStaleShifts` | manual or external cron (the in-process janitor already runs every 60 s) |
| POST (solver) | `/optimize-dispatch` on `SOLVER_URL` | `X-Solver-Token` | `optimize_dispatch` in `apps/solver/dispatch_solver.py` | `callDispatchSolver` in the dispatch job |
| POST (solver) | `/route-geometry` on `SOLVER_URL` | `X-Solver-Token` | OSRM polyline with straight-line fallback | `callRouteGeometry` for the plan map |
| GET (solver) | `/ready` on `SOLVER_URL` | `X-Solver-Token` | (audit PR4) the token check only; `{ ok, version, max_concurrent_dispatch, routing }` | `checkDispatchReadiness` for `/api/health` |

Environment variables used on this path: `SOLVER_URL`, `SOLVER_TOKEN`, `OSRM_URL` (solver side; the workbook assumptions report whether the web has one), `JANITOR_TOKEN`, `ROUTEIQ_DISABLE_JANITOR`, `SOLVER_MAX_CONCURRENT` (web) and `MAX_CONCURRENT_DISPATCH` (solver).

### 3.17 Legacy and unused surfaces still in the repo

RouteIQ started as a generic multi-tenant route planner (the `CLAUDE.md` v1.3 specification, with a PyVRP solver). The NMWC dispatch planner (PR #25, `675f3fc`) replaced its planning flow but kept most of the old pages and routes.

| Surface | Where | How it relates to the dispatch flow |
|---|---|---|
| Plan history (`/t/[slug]/runs`, menu "Plan history") | `apps/web/app/t/[slug]/runs/page.tsx` | Lists every `RunPlan`: all versions, old and new |
| Old run page (`/t/[slug]/runs/[id]`) | `runs/[id]/page.tsx`, `run-detail.tsx`, `scenario-cards.tsx`, `routes-tab.tsx`, `map-tab.tsx`, `baseline-tab.tsx` | Redirects to `/t/[slug]/dispatch/plan/[id]` when the plan has loads, a RECOMMENDED scenario or a version above 1. Otherwise it shows the old per-truck view of legacy plans, made by the PyVRP optimizer in May 2026, before the Sep 2026 restart. The predicate is `isDispatchPlan` (`lib/dispatch/legacy-runs.ts`, PR5), shared with the legacy run routes. Its Optimize button calls `POST /api/runs/:id/optimize`, which now runs the dispatch optimizer; the page then redirects. A 409 `LOCATION_REQUIRED` or `WEIGHT_REQUIRED` asks the same question as the day screen (`askOverride`) and sends the request again with the override, so a new-run DRAFT is not a dead end on days with unknown weights |
| New run (`/t/[slug]/runs/new`, `POST /api/runs`) | `runs/new/page.tsx`, `apps/web/app/api/runs/route.ts` | **PR5: the page redirects to Daily dispatch** (its form is deleted; the dashboard and Plan history buttons open Daily dispatch). `POST /api/runs` creates version 1 through `createInitialPlan` under the day lock (PR3), or returns the live plan for that depot and date |
| Old run actions | `POST /api/runs/:id/dispatch` and `/unlock`; `PATCH` and `DELETE /api/runs/:id/routes/:assignmentId` (`apps/web/lib/route-adjust.ts`); `/api/runs/:id/baseline`; `/api/runs/:id/route-geometries` | Whole-run dispatch and unlock, and moving, locking or removing single stops, answer 409 for dispatch plans (`isDispatchPlan`: loads, a RECOMMENDED option or a later version; PR5). The whole-run dispatch of a legacy run also answers 409 `LOCATION_REQUIRED`, dispatching nothing, while a customer on the run has no correct location (`locationBlocksDelivery` with the customers as they are now; owner's location rule, audit PR A5). Baseline upload (`ManualBaseline`) and route geometries serve only the old run page. Since PR5 route geometries go through the solver's private `/route-geometry` per truck (never Mapbox or OSRM from the web: `lib/road-routing.ts` is deleted, review F22) and a dispatch plan answers 409 `USE_LOAD_GEOMETRY` |
| Upload orders page (`/t/[slug]/upload`, menu "Upload orders") | `apps/web/app/t/[slug]/upload/*` | Older upload screen, still in the menu and on the dashboard. It posts to the same `POST /api/orders/upload` without `depotId` or `deliveryDate` (so: the company's only active depot, tomorrow; since audit PR A5 a company with no active depot, or with two or more, gets 422 `DEPOT_REQUIRED` there and uses Daily dispatch, where the depot is chosen; before, the first active depot by code was taken), shows the new validation JSON, confirms through the same route (asking for a late reason in a prompt), and can delete a batch with `DELETE /api/orders/:batchId`, which the dispatch screen cannot. Sample files come from `GET /api/orders/sample` |
| PyVRP optimizer | `apps/solver/solver.py` and `POST /optimize` in `apps/solver/main.py` | Unreachable from the web app ([4.14](#414-legacy-pyvrp-optimize-path)); the web side (`scheduleOptimize`, `buildSolverPayload`, `callSolver`) was removed in PR5, and the solver endpoint stays until the owner retires it. Old plans it made are recognised by `isLegacyPlan` / `isDispatchDetails` and shown read-only |
| Driver PWA (**retired in PR1**) | `apps/web/app/driver/*` (a notice now), `apps/web/app/api/driver/{login,manifest,ping,stop,shift/end}`, `POST /api/drivers/:id/pin` and `/api/runs/:id/live` (all 410, `lib/driver-app.ts`); `apps/web/lib/driver-auth.ts` and `lib/jobs/shift-janitor.ts` kept until the tables are dropped | Retired by owner decision: the review's F12, F14 and PIN findings are moot. Before that it was not adapted to loads. The manifest picks the latest READY or DISPATCHED run with assignments on the truck and orders its stops by `sequenceInTruck` only, which restarts at 1 for every load, so a truck's trips would interleave. Delivery proofs do not change order or load status. The live page is linked only from the old run page. The dispatch flow uses the PDF driver sheets and WhatsApp instead |
| Regions | `/t/[slug]/regions`, `/api/regions`, `Region`, `Customer.regionId` | Master data only; nothing in `apps/web/lib/dispatch` reads regions |
| Signup and onboarding | `/signup` with `POST /api/auth/signup` (creates a tenant and its TENANT_ADMIN; open by owner decision, `SIGNUP_MODE=closed` turns it off), `/t/[slug]/onboard` wizard (depots, trucks) | From the SaaS specification. Not part of the NMWC daily flow; the NMWC tenant is seeded with `pnpm db:seed:dispatch` |
| Dashboard (`/t/[slug]`) | `apps/web/app/t/[slug]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/lib/dashboard.ts` | KPIs from each day's plan in use: since PR5 from its plan summary (every load of the version, so a re-planned day counts its carried locked loads too), on Asia/Muscat days. Its button opens Daily dispatch |

---

## 4. Optimizer logic

The optimizer is the Python service in `apps/solver`, a FastAPI app (`apps/solver/main.py`). It builds the NMWC daily plan in two stages:

1. **Route search.** Google OR-Tools vehicle routing, in `apps/solver/dispatch_solver.py`. It decides which stops go together in a load and in what order.
2. **Post-solve stage.** OR-Tools CP-SAT plus small GLOP linear programs, in `apps/solver/load_repack.py`. It re-assigns whole loads to trucks and departure times, times every plan exactly, and picks one plan per scenario.

The engine calls its result an *OPTIMIZED* plan: good and feasible within a time limit, never claimed to be a proven optimum. Two docs explain it in plain language: `docs/OPTIMIZER_DESIGN.md` covers the model and `docs/OPTIMIZER_BENCHMARK.md` covers the engine choice and the measurements.

### 4.1 Files and entry points

| File | Role | Key functions / classes |
|---|---|---|
| `apps/solver/main.py` | HTTP API. Every endpoint except `GET /health` needs the `X-Solver-Token` header, compared with the `SOLVER_TOKEN` env var | `optimize_dispatch_endpoint` (`POST /optimize-dispatch`), `route_geometry_endpoint` (`POST /route-geometry`), `optimize_endpoint` (legacy `POST /optimize`), `health`, `routing_status`, `_check_token` |
| `apps/solver/dispatch_models.py` | Pydantic wire contract | `DispatchRequest`, `DispatchConfig`, `DispatchTruck`, `DispatchStop`, `FrozenTrip`, `DispatchResponse`, `DispatchScenario`, `PlannedLoad`, `PlannedStop`, `UnservedStop`, `ObjectiveComponents`, `UnservedReason` |
| `apps/solver/providers.py` | Distance and time matrix | `resolve_matrix`, `OSRMProvider`, `HaversineProvider`, `MatrixResult`, `configured_osrm_url` |
| `apps/solver/dispatch_solver.py` | Truck-days, prefilters, the OR-Tools model, scenarios, worker pool, extraction, post-solve orchestration | `optimize_dispatch`, `_truck_days`, `_prefilter`, `_window_prefilter`, `_solve_scenario`, `_service_values`, `_drop_penalties`, `_initial_assignment`, `_run_scenarios`, `_post_solve`, `_build_scenario`, `_assert_reconciled` |
| `apps/solver/load_repack.py` | Post-solve stage | `facts`, `depart_range`, `repack`, `time_truck`, `time_plan`, `timing_ok`, `score`, `build_candidates`, `fit_pool` |
| `packages/shared-types/src/dispatch.ts` | TypeScript mirror of the contract | same names as above |
| `apps/web/lib/dispatch/plan-service.ts` | Builds the request from the database | `buildDispatchRequest`, `usesPlanContinuity` |
| `apps/web/lib/dispatch/split.ts`, `apps/web/lib/dispatch/service-time.ts` | Split deliveries, service time per stop | `choosePartCapacity`, `splitIntoParts`, `stopServiceMin` |
| `apps/web/lib/solver-client.ts` | HTTP client | `callDispatchSolver`, `postJsonLong`, `callRouteGeometry`; legacy `callSolver` |
| `apps/solver/solver.py`, `models.py`, `distance.py` | Legacy PyVRP `/optimize` path ([4.14](#414-legacy-pyvrp-optimize-path)) | `optimize` |
| `apps/solver/tests/test_dispatch.py`, `test_repack.py` | Tests for the dispatch engine: 66 and 31 test functions (PR6 review) | e.g. `test_strict_priority_one_p2_beats_eleven_p3`, `test_repack_reduces_trucks_on_a_day_the_search_spreads_out` |
| `apps/solver/scripts/bench_dispatch.py` | Synthetic benchmark: `cd apps/solver && .venv/Scripts/python scripts/bench_dispatch.py 150` | uses `tests.test_dispatch.nmwc_day` |
| `apps/solver/scripts/bench_replan.py` | Re-plan benchmark around locked loads, two solver versions on the same requests (`state`, `run [--root <other checkout>/apps/solver]`, `compare`; see its docstring) | [`OPTIMIZER_BENCHMARK.md`](./OPTIMIZER_BENCHMARK.md) section 9.2 |

### 4.2 Solve pipeline

```mermaid
flowchart TD
    W["Web: plan-service.buildDispatchRequest<br/>split.ts, service-time.ts"] -->|"POST /optimize-dispatch<br/>solver-client.callDispatchSolver, 600 s timeout"| A
    A["main.optimize_dispatch_endpoint<br/>token check + pydantic validation"] --> B["dispatch_solver.optimize_dispatch"]
    B --> C["_truck_days<br/>earliest departure, latest return, trips left"]
    C --> D["_prefilter, no matrix yet<br/>NO_AVAILABLE_TRUCK, TRIP_LIMIT, SHIFT_LIMIT,<br/>EXCEEDS_ANY_TRUCK_CAPACITY"]
    D --> E["providers.resolve_matrix<br/>OSRM /table blocks, else Haversine"]
    E --> F["_window_prefilter<br/>HARD_WINDOW_INFEASIBLE, SHIFT_LIMIT"]
    F --> G["_submatrix + auto_time_limit"]
    G --> H["_run_scenarios<br/>spawn worker pool, 540 s request budget"]
    H --> R["RECOMMENDED: _solve_scenario<br/>full time limit"]
    R -->|"raw loads as warm start"| T["MIN_TRUCKS: _solve_scenario<br/>half limit"]
    R -->|"raw loads as warm start"| M["MIN_DISTANCE: _solve_scenario<br/>half limit"]
    R --> P
    T --> P
    M --> P
    P["_post_solve -> load_repack.build_candidates<br/>exact LP timing, CP-SAT repack,<br/>drop repair, fit fallback, score"] --> S["pick per scenario with _GOALS<br/>_build_scenario + _assert_reconciled"]
    S --> O["DispatchResponse"]
    O -->|"persistDispatchResult + applyScenario RECOMMENDED"| DB["Web database"]
```

Inside each `_solve_scenario` call, the steps are: build the routing model, run the search (warm or cold), convert the assignment to loads (`_extract` → `_timed_from_assignment`), and turn them into a scenario (`_build_scenario`).

To run the solver tests locally (from `apps/solver/README.md`): `.venv/Scripts/python -m pytest tests -q`.

### 4.3 Request and response contract

`apps/solver/dispatch_models.py` is the source of truth. `packages/shared-types/src/dispatch.ts` mirrors its `/optimize-dispatch` models field for field; on the TypeScript side every `DispatchConfig` field is optional, so the solver defaults apply. Units:

- **Times** are minutes from local midnight of the delivery day (06:30 = 390). `DAY_MIN = 1440`. Frozen trips and `available_to_min` may run up to 2 × 1440.
- **Money** is OMR. **Response distances** are km.

**Request** (`DispatchRequest`: `run_id`, `tenant_id`, `depot`, `trucks[]`, `stops[]`, `config`):

| Object | Fields (solver default) | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| `DispatchDepot` | `id`, `lat`, `lng`, `open_min` (0), `close_min` (1440) | |
| `DispatchTruck` | `capacity_cases`, `capacity_kg` (0 = weight not limited), `fixed_cost` per day used, `trip_cost` per load, `cost_per_km` (non-fuel), `km_per_litre`, `available_from_min`, `available_to_min`, `max_trips` (1–10), `frozen_trips[]` | `frozen_trips` = every non-PLANNED load of the truck in this run (LOCKED, LOADING, DISPATCHED and COMPLETED; the web queries `status: { not: 'PLANNED' }`), with `load_no`, `depart_min`, `return_min`, `cases`. The Pydantic `FrozenTrip` docstring names all four since PR4. The solver never changes them; they only block truck time |
| `DispatchStop` | `stop_id`, `order_ids[]` (min 1), `customer_id`, `lat`, `lng`, `demand_cases`, `demand_kg`, `service_min` (10, max 480), `priority` (3; 1 = highest, 5 = lowest), `hard_start_min`/`hard_end_min`, `pref_start_min`/`pref_end_min`, `margin`, `revenue`, `late`, `previous_truck_id` | One stop = one customer branch, or one part of a split delivery. The validator `_windows_ordered` rejects windows that end before they start |
| `DispatchConfig` | `shift_start_min` (360), `shift_max_min` (660), `overtime_after_min` (540), `overtime_cost_per_hour` (4.0), `reload_min` (30), `loading_min_per_case` (0, max 1), `loading_from_min` (None; a plan made on its delivery day, PR8 review), `max_trips_per_truck` (3), `fuel_price_per_litre` (0), `driver_cost_per_hour` (0), `strict_priorities` (true), `priority_weights` ({1:10000, 2:1000, 3:100, 4:10, 5:1}), `pref_window_penalty_per_min` (0.05), `early_preference_per_min` ({1:0.01, 2:0.005, 3–5:0}), `use_margin` (true), `change_penalty_per_stop` (3.0), `distance_provider` ("OSRM"), `osrm_url`, `haversine_multiplier` (1.3), `avg_speed_kmh` (40), `road_time_factor` (1.25), `time_limit_sec` (None = automatic), `scenarios` (all three) | The validator `_priority_monotonic` requires P1 > P2 > … > P5 > 0 even when strict priorities are on |

`DispatchRequest._unique_ids` rejects duplicate `stop_id` values, an order id that appears in two stops, and duplicate truck ids. Since PR5 `stops` has `max_length` **600** (`MAX_STOPS`): more answer 422; the web refuses them first with 422 `TOO_MANY_STOPS`. Above 350 stops the optimizer adds a "Large day" warning (`dispatch_solver.LARGE_DAY_STOPS`, shown with the plan's warnings); the web adds none of its own. The web's bounds for every setting it sends are in `packages/shared-types/src/planner-bounds.json`, inside these Pydantic ranges.

**Response** (`DispatchResponse`):

- Top level: `run_id`, `engine` (`"ortools-routing"`), `matrix_provider` (`OSRM` or `HAVERSINE`), `distance_is_estimated` (true only when **every** leg is an estimate), `distance_quality` (PR5: `ROAD` / `MIXED` / `ESTIMATED`), `scenarios[]`, `warnings[]`.
- Each `DispatchScenario` has:
  - `name`, `status` (`OPTIMIZED` / `NO_SOLUTION` / `NOTHING_TO_PLAN`), `solver_status` (the OR-Tools status name, stored as is; since PR6 every screen, sheet and message shows it in plain words through `solverStatusText` in `apps/web/lib/dispatch/solver-status.ts`), `solver_time_sec`, `time_limit_sec`, `objective_value`;
  - `objective`: an `ObjectiveComponents` breakdown in OMR;
  - totals: `trucks_used`, `trips`, km, minutes, cases, kg, average utilisation, fuel, `operating_cost`. `trucks_used` is the day's **physical** trucks with this plan: the trucks of its new loads + every truck with frozen (locked, loading, dispatched, completed) loads, whether or not it gets a new load (PR7, B3; before, only the new loads' trucks, so a re-plan understated the trucks). `trips` stays the new loads. PR7 adds the optional `frozen_trucks` and `frozen_loads` (the part of the day kept as it was; absent from an older solver);
  - `loads[]` (`PlannedLoad` with `stops[]` of `PlannedStop`), `unserved[]` (`UnservedStop` with `reason_code` and `reason_message`) and `warnings[]`;
  - `feasibility` (PR4, optional in both directions: an older web ignores it, an older solver sends none): `FeasibilityReport { status: VERIFIED | VIOLATED | UNVERIFIED, timing: EXACT | ESTIMATED, violations[{ code, truck_id, load_no, stop_id, message, short_by_min }], checked_at_version, travel_checked, note }` from `feasibility.check_scenario` ([4.11](#411-post-solve-stage));
  - (PR5, all optional in both directions) the cost model: `cost_policy` (`TRUCK_DAY_SPAN`), `cost_version` (2), `truck_days[]` (per truck: loads, frozen loads, `day_start_min`, `paid_from_min`, `last_return_min`, `paid_min`, `overtime_min` and the money by kind, each the sum of its loads), `paid_driver_min`, `preference_penalties { window, early, continuity }` (OMR-equivalent, not money) and `estimated_legs`. Each `PlannedLoad` adds `trip_cost`, `driver_cost` (= `time_cost`), `overtime_cost`, `driver_paid_min`, `paid_from_min`, `overtime_min` and `estimated_legs`; each `PlannedStop` adds `leg_estimated`. With `cost_version` 2, `fixed_cost` is the truck's day cost alone (before, it included the trip cost) and `total_cost` includes the whole-day driver share and overtime ([4.10](#410-extraction-and-reported-costs)).

### 4.4 How the web shapes the request

What `buildDispatchRequest` puts in each stop and truck is in the table in [3.6](#36-step-4-optimize). Two more details matter for the optimizer: the config mapping and the split algorithm. The margin rule also matters: the solver uses margins only if *every* stop has one (`use_margin` in `_solve_scenario`).

**Config mapping** (from `TenantConfig` in `apps/web/prisma/schema.prisma`; since PR5 one pure function, `dispatchConfigFromTenant` in `lib/dispatch/planner-config.ts`, which also feeds Settings' effective values panel, [3.12](#312-settings-that-change-the-plan)):

| Request field | Tenant field (DB default) |
|---|---|
| `shift_start_min` | `shiftStartMin` (360); for a plan built on its own delivery day, now + `reloadMinutes` when that is later than `shiftStartMin` and the depot opening (PR8, `sameDayPlanFrom` in `lib/dispatch/plan-from.ts`, set in `buildDispatchRequest` after the mapping; the Settings panel shows the setting) |
| `shift_max_min` | `driverShiftMaxMinutes` (540) |
| `overtime_after_min` / `overtime_cost_per_hour` | `overtimeAfterMin` (540) / `overtimeCostPerHour` (0) |
| `reload_min` / `loading_min_per_case` | `reloadMinutes` (30) / `loadingMinPerCase` (0; `lib/schemas.ts` allows 0–1) |
| `loading_from_min` | not a setting: the time the plan is made, sent only when it is built on its own delivery day (PR8 review, `planDayNowMin` in `lib/dispatch/plan-from.ts`, set in `buildDispatchRequest`; kept as `PlanSettings.loadingFromMin`). Loading of new loads starts then at the earliest ([3.11](#311-step-9-late-orders-and-plan-versions)) |
| `max_trips_per_truck` | `maxTripsPerTruck` (3) |
| `fuel_price_per_litre`, `driver_cost_per_hour` | `fuelPricePerLitre` (0), `driverCostPerHour` (0) |
| `strict_priorities` | always `true` (hard-coded) |
| `priority_weights` | `parsePriorityWeights(priorityWeightsJson)` (only used when strict is off) |
| `pref_window_penalty_per_min` | `prefWindowPenaltyPerMin` (0.05) |
| `use_margin` | always `true` |
| `distance_provider` | `routingProviderFor` (`lib/dispatch/customer-attrs.ts`): HAVERSINE if the tenant chose it, or if there is no tenant `osrmUrl` and the country is not Oman/UAE; otherwise OSRM |
| `osrm_url`, `haversine_multiplier`, `avg_speed_kmh`, `road_time_factor` | `osrmUrl`, `distanceMultiplier` (1.30), `avgSpeedKmh` (40), `roadTimeFactor` (1.25) |
| `time_limit_sec` | always `null` (automatic). `TenantConfig.solverTimeLimitSeconds` is not sent |
